018603:   function affine(s0,s1,s2,d0,d1,d2){
018604:     const sx1=s1.x-s0.x,sy1=s1.y-s0.y,sx2=s2.x-s0.x,sy2=s2.y-s0.y,
018605:           dx1=d1.x-d0.x,dy1=d1.y-d0.y,dx2=d2.x-d0.x,dy2=d2.y-d0.y,det=sx1*sy2-sx2*sy1;

019742:   // Maps treatment concept + active chief complaint cards → not just Support/Context
019743:   // *extent* but also the directional *shape* of the ROI (horizontal widening toward
019744:   // the commissures, vertical extension toward the gingiva or the incisal edge).
019745:   // This is what makes each card change the ROI's FORM, not only a magnitude value.
019746:   function cfSimROIConfig(concept, targets){
019747:     // radial extent multipliers — context defines the outermost falloff radius
019748:     let support=1.55, context=2.15;
019749:     // directional shape bias (neutral = 1.0 / 0.0)

020223:           const kb=clamp(kb0*m,0,0.5);  r+=(255-r)*kb*0.88; g+=(255-g)*kb*0.94; b+=(255-b)*kb;
020224:           // Aging direction (opposite of whitening): ADD chroma toward a warmer, more saturated
020225:           // amber shade and lower the value more strongly.
020226:           if(shadeAge>0){
020227:             const kc=clamp(kc0*m,0,0.6);
020228:             r+=(r-lum)*kc + 7*kc;   // push away from grey + warm boost
020229:             g+=(g-lum)*kc + 2*kc;
020230:             b+=(b-lum)*kc - 8*kc;   // reduce blue => warmer / more yellow-amber
